### rich.console.Console

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Documentation for the rich.console.Console class, including its constructor parameters and methods.

```APIDOC
## Console Class

### Description
A high level console interface for rendering rich text, logging, and capturing output.

### Constructor
`Console(*, color_system='auto', force_terminal=None, force_jupyter=None, force_interactive=None, soft_wrap=False, theme=None, stderr=False, file=None, quiet=False, width=None, height=None, style=None, no_color=None, tab_size=8, record=False, markup=True, emoji=True, emoji_variant=None, highlight=True, log_time=True, log_path=True, log_time_format='[%X]', highlighter=<rich.highlighter.ReprHighlighter object>, legacy_windows=None, safe_box=True, get_datetime=None, get_time=None, _environ=None)`

### Parameters
- **color_system** (str, optional) - The color system supported by your terminal, either `"standard"`, `"256"` or `"truecolor"`. Leave as `"auto"` to autodetect.
- **force_terminal** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable/disable terminal control codes, or None to auto-detect terminal. Defaults to None.
- **force_jupyter** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable/disable Jupyter rendering, or None to auto-detect Jupyter. Defaults to None.
- **force_interactive** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable/disable interactive mode, or None to auto detect. Defaults to None.
- **soft_wrap** (Optional[bool], optional) - Set soft wrap default on print method. Defaults to False.
- **theme** (Theme, optional) - An optional style theme object, or `None` for default theme.
- **stderr** (bool, optional) - Use stderr rather than stdout if `file` is not specified. Defaults to False.
- **file** (IO, optional) - A file object where the console should write to. Defaults to stdout.
- **quiet** (bool, Optional) - Boolean to suppress all output. Defaults to False.
- **width** (int, optional) - The width of the terminal. Leave as default to auto-detect width.
- **height** (int, optional) - The height of the terminal. Leave as default to auto-detect height.
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style to apply to all output, or None for no style. Defaults to None.
- **no_color** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enabled no color mode, or None to auto detect. Defaults to None.
- **tab_size** (int, optional) - Number of spaces used to replace a tab character. Defaults to 8.
- **record** (bool, optional) - Boolean to enable recording of terminal output, required to call `export_html()`, `export_svg()`, and `export_text()`. Defaults to False.
- **markup** (bool, optional) - Boolean to enable Console Markup. Defaults to True.
- **emoji** (bool, optional) - Enable emoji code. Defaults to True.
- **emoji_variant** (str, optional) - Optional emoji variant, either "text" or "emoji". Defaults to None.
- **highlight** (bool, optional) - Enable automatic highlighting. Defaults to True.
- **log_time** (bool, optional) - Boolean to enable logging of time by `log()` methods. Defaults to True.
- **log_path** (bool, optional) - Boolean to enable the logging of the caller by `log()`. Defaults to True.
- **log_time_format** (Union[str, TimeFormatterCallable], optional) - If `log_time` is enabled, either string for strftime or callable that formats the time. Defaults to "[%X] ".
- **highlighter** (HighlighterType, optional) - Default highlighter.
- **legacy_windows** (bool, optional) - Enable legacy Windows mode, or `None` to auto detect. Defaults to `None`.
- **safe_box** (bool, optional) - Restrict box options that don't render on legacy Windows.
- **get_datetime** (Callable[[], datetime], optional) - Callable that gets the current time as a datetime.datetime object (used by Console.log), or None for datetime.now.
- **get_time** (Callable[[], time], optional) - Callable that gets the current time in seconds, default uses time.monotonic.
- **_environ** (Mapping[str, str]) - Environment mapping.

### Methods

#### begin_capture()

Begin capturing console output. Call `end_capture()` to exit capture mode and return output.

**Return type:** None

#### bell()

Play a 'bell' sound (if supported by the terminal).

**Return type:** None

#### capture()

A context manager to *capture* the result of print() or log() in a string, rather than writing it to the console.

**Example:**
```pycon
>>> from rich.console import Console
>>> console = Console()
>>> with console.capture() as capture:
...     console.print("[bold magenta]Hello World[/]")
>>> print(capture.get())
```

**Returns:** Context manager with disables writing to the terminal.
**Return type:** Capture
```

--------------------------------

### Description

## Console.input(prompt='', markup=True, emoji=True, password=False, stream=None)

### Description

Function

- **emoji** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable emoji (requires a str prompt). Defaults to True.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Hide typed text. Defaults to False.

### Return Type

```

--------------------------------

### Align a panel to the center

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/align.rst

Creates a 20-character-wide panel and renders it centered in the terminal using Align with align="center". Requires importing Console, Align, and Panel.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.align import Align
from rich.panel import Panel

console = Console()
# Create a panel 20 characters wide
p = Panel("Hello, [b]World[/b]!", style="on green", width=20)

# Renders the panel centered in the terminal
console.print(Align(p, align="center"))
```

### Spinner(name, text='', style=None, speed=1.0)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/spinner.rst

Creates a new Spinner instance with the specified name, text, style, and speed. Raises KeyError if the name is not a supported spinner animation.

```APIDOC
## Spinner(name, text='', style=None, speed=1.0)

### Description
Creates a new Spinner instance with the specified name, text, style, and speed. Raises KeyError if the name is not a supported spinner animation.

### Parameters
- **name** (str) - Required - Name of spinner (run python -m rich.spinner).
- **text** (RenderableType, optional) - A renderable to display at the right of the spinner (str or Text typically). Defaults to "".
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for spinner animation. Defaults to None.
- **speed** (float, optional) - Speed factor for animation. Defaults to 1.0.

### Raises
- **KeyError** - If name isn't one of the supported spinner animations.
```

### Customizing default styles

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/style.rst

Shows how to override built-in Rich styles by including them in a custom Theme.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.theme import Theme
console = Console(theme=Theme({"repr.number": "bold green blink"}))
console.print("The total is 128")
```

## TaskProgressColumn

### Description
Shows task progress as a percentage.

### Constructor Parameters
- **text_format** (str, optional) - Format for percentage display. Defaults to "[progress.percentage]{task.percentage:>3.0f}%".
- **text_format_no_percentage** (str, optional) - Format if percentage is unknown. Defaults to "".
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style of output. Defaults to "none".
- **justify** (JustifyMethod, optional) - Text justification. Defaults to "left".
- **markup** (bool, optional) - Enable markup. Defaults to True.
- **highlighter** (Optional[Highlighter], optional) - Highlighter to apply to output. Defaults to None.
- **table_column** (Optional[Column], optional) - Table Column to use. Defaults to None.
- **show_speed** (bool, optional) - Show speed if total is unknown. Defaults to False.

### Methods

Should return a renderable object.
- **Parameters:** task (Task)
- **Return type:** Text

#### render_speed(speed) (classmethod)
Render the speed in iterations per second.
- **Parameters:** task (Task), speed (float | None)
- **Returns:** Text object containing the task speed.
- **Return type:** Text
```

### rich.style.Style

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/style.rst

Documentation for the rich.style.Style class, including its constructor, properties, and methods.

```APIDOC
## Style Class

### Description
Represents a terminal style with color, background color, and attributes like bold, italic, etc. Attributes can be on (True), off (False), or not set (None).

### Constructor
`Style(color=None, bgcolor=None, bold=None, dim=None, italic=None, underline=None, blink=None, blink2=None, reverse=None, conceal=None, strike=None, underline2=None, frame=None, encircle=None, overline=None, link=None, meta=None)`

### Parameters
- **color** (Union[Color, str], optional) - Color of terminal text. Defaults to None.
- **bgcolor** (Union[Color, str], optional) - Color of terminal background. Defaults to None.
- **bold** (bool, optional) - Enable bold text. Defaults to None.
- **dim** (bool, optional) - Enable dim text. Defaults to None.
- **italic** (bool, optional) - Enable italic text. Defaults to None.
- **underline** (bool, optional) - Enable underlined text. Defaults to None.
- **blink** (bool, optional) - Enabled blinking text. Defaults to None.
- **blink2** (bool, optional) - Enable fast blinking text. Defaults to None.
- **reverse** (bool, optional) - Enabled reverse text. Defaults to None.
- **conceal** (bool, optional) - Enable concealed text. Defaults to None.
- **strike** (bool, optional) - Enable strikethrough text. Defaults to None.
- **underline2** (bool, optional) - Enable doubly underlined text. Defaults to None.
- **frame** (bool, optional) - Enable framed text. Defaults to None.
- **encircle** (bool, optional) - Enable encircled text. Defaults to None.
- **overline** (bool, optional) - Enable overlined text. Defaults to None.
- **link** (str, optional) - Link URL. Defaults to None.
- **meta** (Dict[str, Any] | None) - Meta information.

### Properties
- **background_style** (Style) - A Style with background only.
- **bgcolor** (Color | None) - The background color or None if it is not set.
- **color** (Color | None) - The foreground color or None if it is not set.
- **link** (str | None) - Link text, if set.
- **link_id** (str) - Get a link id, used in ansi code for links.
- **meta** (Dict[str, Any]) - Get meta information (can not be changed after construction).

### Methods

#### chain(\*styles)
Combine styles from positional argument in to a single style.

**Parameters:**
- **\*styles** (Iterable[Style]) - Styles to combine.

**Returns:**
- A new style instance.

#### clear_meta_and_links()
Get a copy of this style with link and meta information removed.

**Returns:**
- New style object.

#### combine(styles)
Combine styles and get result.

**Parameters:**
- **styles** (Iterable[Style]) - Styles to combine.

**Returns:**
- A new style instance.

#### copy()
Get a copy of this style.

**Returns:**
- A new Style instance with identical attributes.

#### from_color(color=None, bgcolor=None)
Create a new style with colors and no attributes.

**Parameters:**
- **color** (Optional[Color]) - A (foreground) color, or None for no color. Defaults to None.
- **bgcolor** (Optional[Color]) - A (background) color, or None for no color. Defaults to None.

**Returns:**
- A new style instance.

#### from_meta(meta)
Create a new style with meta data.

**Parameters:**
- **meta** (Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) - A dictionary of meta data. Defaults to None.

**Returns:**
- A new style instance.

#### get_html_style(theme=None)
Get a CSS style rule.

**Parameters:**
- **theme** (TerminalTheme | None) - Optional theme.

**Returns:**
- str - CSS style rule.

#### normalize(style)
Normalize a style definition so that styles with the same effect have the same string representation.

**Parameters:**
- **style** (str) - A style definition.

**Returns:**
- str - Normal form of style definition.

#### null()
Create an 'null' style, equivalent to Style(), but more performant.

**Returns:**
- Style - A new Style instance.

#### on(meta=None, \*\*handlers)
Create a blank style with meta information.

**Parameters:**
- **meta** (Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) - Meta data.
- **\*\*handlers** - Additional keyword arguments.

**Returns:**
- Style - A new Style instance.
```

### Console.print

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Prints objects to the console with rich formatting options.

```APIDOC
## Console.print

### Description
Print to the console. This method provides rich formatting options including justification, overflow handling, emoji, markup, and highlighting.

### Method
print

### Signature
`print(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', style=None, justify=None, overflow=None, no_wrap=None, emoji=None, markup=None, highlight=None, width=None, height=None, crop=True, soft_wrap=None, new_line_start=False)`

### Parameters
- **objects** (*positional args*) - Required - Objects to log to the terminal.
- **sep** (*str*, optional) - String to write between print data. Defaults to " ".
- **end** (*str*, optional) - String to write at end of print data. Defaults to "\n".
- **style** (*Union[str, Style]*, optional) - A style to apply to output. Defaults to None.
- **justify** (*str*, optional) - Justify method: "default", "left", "right", "center", or "full". Defaults to `None`.
- **overflow** (*str*, optional) - Overflow method: "ignore", "crop", "fold", or "ellipsis". Defaults to None.
- **no_wrap** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Disable word wrapping. Defaults to None.
- **emoji** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Enable emoji code, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to `None`.
- **markup** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Enable markup, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to `None`.
- **highlight** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Enable automatic highlighting, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to `None`.
- **width** (*Optional[int]*, optional) - Width of output, or `None` to auto-detect. Defaults to `None`.
- **height** (*int | None*) - Height of output, or `None` to auto-detect.
- **crop** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Crop output to width of terminal. Defaults to True.
- **soft_wrap** (*bool*, optional) - Enable soft wrap mode which disables word wrapping and cropping of text or `None` for Console default. Defaults to `None`.
- **new_line_start** (*bool*, optional) - Insert a new line at the start if the output contains more than one line. Defaults to `False`.

### Return Type
None
```

### Traceback.from_exception

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/traceback.rst

Class method to create a Traceback instance from exception information.

```APIDOC
## Traceback.from_exception(exc_type, exc_value, traceback, width=100, code_width=88, extra_lines=3, theme=None, word_wrap=False, show_locals=False, locals_max_length=10, locals_max_string=80, locals_max_depth=None, locals_hide_dunder=True, locals_hide_sunder=False, locals_overflow=None, indent_guides=True, suppress=(), max_frames=100)

### Description
Creates a Traceback instance from exception information. The resulting instance can be printed to display a formatted traceback with syntax highlighting and rich formatting.

### Method
classmethod

### Parameters
- **exc_type** (Type[BaseException]) - Required - Exception type.
- **exc_value** (BaseException) - Required - Exception value.
- **traceback** (TracebackType) - Required - Python Traceback object.
- **width** (Optional[int], optional) - Optional - Number of characters used to traceback. Defaults to 100.
- **code_width** (Optional[int], optional) - Optional - Number of code characters used to traceback. Defaults to 88.
- **extra_lines** (int, optional) - Optional - Additional lines of code to render. Defaults to 3.
- **theme** (str, optional) - Optional - Override pygments theme used in traceback.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable word wrapping of long lines. Defaults to False.
- **show_locals** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable display of local variables. Defaults to False.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable indent guides in code and locals. Defaults to True.
- **locals_max_length** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum length of containers before abbreviating, or None for no abbreviation. Defaults to 10.
- **locals_max_depth** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum depths of locals before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **locals_max_string** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum length of string before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to 80.
- **locals_hide_dunder** (bool, optional) - Optional - Hide locals prefixed with double underscore. Defaults to True.
- **locals_hide_sunder** (bool, optional) - Optional - Hide locals prefixed with single underscore. Defaults to False.
- **locals_overflow** (OverflowMethod, optional) - Optional - How to handle overflowing locals, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **suppress** (Iterable[Union[str, ModuleType]]) - Optional - Optional sequence of modules or paths to exclude from traceback.
- **max_frames** (int) - Optional - Maximum number of frames to show in a traceback, 0 for no maximum. Defaults to 100.

### Returns
A Traceback instance that may be printed.

### Return Type
Traceback
```

### Basic usage of rich.progress.track

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/progress.rst

Use track() to iterate over a sequence while displaying a progress bar. The description parameter labels the task. Each iteration updates the progress; time.sleep simulates work.

```default
import time
from rich.progress import track

for i in track(range(20), description="Processing..."):
    time.sleep(1)  # Simulate work being done
```

--------------------------------

### Demonstrate overflow methods with Console.print

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Demonstrates the fold, crop, and ellipsis overflow methods using a Console with width=14. The default is 'fold', which wraps excess characters to new lines; 'crop' truncates at the line end; 'ellipsis' truncates and appends an ellipsis character. The output shows each method's effect on the long word 'supercalifragilisticexpialidocious'.

```python
from typing import List
from rich.console import Console, OverflowMethod

console = Console(width=14)
supercali = "supercalifragilisticexpialidocious"

overflow_methods: List[OverflowMethod] = ["fold", "crop", "ellipsis"]
for overflow in overflow_methods:
    console.rule(overflow)
    console.print(supercali, overflow=overflow, style="bold blue")
    console.print()
```

### Log with Console.log and log_locals

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Demonstrates the Console.log() method, which renders a column for the current time and the file/line of the call. By default, Rich does syntax highlighting for Python structures and repr strings, and pretty-prints collections. The log_locals argument outputs a table of local variables where log was called.

```python
from rich.console import Console
console = Console()

test_data = [
    {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "sum", "params": [None, 1, 2, 4, False, True], "id": "1",},
    {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notify_hello", "params": [7]},
    {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "subtract", "params": [42, 23], "id": "2"},
]

def test_log():
    enabled = False
    context = {
        "foo": "bar",
    }
    movies = ["Deadpool", "Rise of the Skywalker"]
    console.log("Hello from", console, "!")
    console.log(test_data, log_locals=True)


test_log()
```

--------------------------------

### Bird class with standard __repr__

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/pretty.rst

A class that might benefit from a Rich repr, with a standard __repr__ method and a dictionary of Bird instances.

```default
class Bird:
    def __init__(self, name, eats=None, fly=True, extinct=False):
        self.name = name
        self.eats = list(eats) if eats else []
        self.fly = fly
        self.extinct = extinct

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Bird({self.name!r}, eats={self.eats!r}, fly={self.fly!r}, extinct={self.extinct!r})"

BIRDS = {
    "gull": Bird("gull", eats=["fish", "chips", "ice cream", "sausage rolls"]),
    "penguin": Bird("penguin", eats=["fish"], fly=False),
    "dodo": Bird("dodo", eats=["fruit"], fly=False, extinct=True)
}
print(BIRDS)
```

--------------------------------

### Display Pretty data inside a Panel

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/pretty.rst

Creates a Pretty instance around locals() and wraps it in a Panel before printing. Useful when you want to show a pretty-printed representation of data inside another Rich renderable.

```python
from rich import print
from rich.pretty import Pretty
from rich.panel import Panel

pretty = Pretty(locals())
panel = Panel(pretty)
print(panel)
```

--------------------------------

### Console.render()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference.rst

### FloatPrompt.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

FloatPrompt is a prompt that returns a float. Its ask method returns a float result.

```APIDOC
## FloatPrompt.ask

### Description
A prompt that returns a float. The ask method is inherited from PromptBase and returns a float result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop. For FloatPrompt, this is a float.

### Example
```pycon
>>> temperature = FloatPrompt.ask("Enter desired temperature")
```
```

### Custom RainbowHighlighter with Highlighter

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/highlighting.rst

Extends the Highlighter base class to implement a custom highlighting scheme. The highlight method is passed a Text object and stylizes each character with a random color from 16 to 255. The highlighter is used as a callable to print the highlighted text.

```python
from random import randint

from rich import print
from rich.highlighter import Highlighter


class RainbowHighlighter(Highlighter):
    def highlight(self, text):
        for index in range(len(text)):
            text.stylize(f"color({randint(16, 255)})", index, index + 1)


rainbow = RainbowHighlighter()
print(rainbow("I must not fear. Fear is the mind-killer."))
```

--------------------------------

### Basic Padding with single value

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/padding.rst

Creates a Padding object with 1 character of padding on all sides, resulting in a blank line above and below and a space on left and right edges.

```default
from rich import print
from rich.padding import Padding
test = Padding("Hello", 1)
print(test)
```

### render_str

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Converts a string to a Text instance. This is called automatically when printing or logging a string.

```APIDOC
## render_str(text, style='', justify=None, overflow=None, emoji=None, markup=None, highlight=None, highlighter=None)

### Description
Converts a string to a Text instance. This is called automatically when printing or logging a string.

### Parameters
- **text** (str) - Required - Text to render.
- **style** (Union[str, Style], optional) - Style to apply to rendered text.
- **justify** (str, optional) - Justify method: "default", "left", "center", "full", or "right". Defaults to `None`.
- **overflow** (str, optional) - Overflow method: "crop", "fold", or "ellipsis". Defaults to `None`.
- **emoji** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable emoji, or `None` to use Console default.
- **markup** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable markup, or `None` to use Console default.
- **highlight** (Optional[bool], optional) - Enable highlighting, or `None` to use Console default.
- **highlighter** (HighlighterType, optional) - Optional highlighter to apply.

### Returns
- **ConsoleRenderable** - Renderable object.
```

### JSON(json, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/json.rst

Constructor for the JSON renderable class that pretty prints JSON encoded data.

```APIDOC
## JSON(json, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

### Description
Creates a renderable that pretty prints JSON encoded data.

### Constructor Parameters
- **json** (str) - Required - JSON encoded data.
- **indent** (Union[None, int, str]) - Optional - Number of characters to indent by. Defaults to 2.
- **highlight** (bool) - Optional - Enable highlighting. Defaults to True.
- **skip_keys** (bool) - Optional - Skip keys not of a basic type. Defaults to False.
- **ensure_ascii** (bool) - Optional - Escape all non-ascii characters. Defaults to False.
- **check_circular** (bool) - Optional - Check for circular references. Defaults to True.
- **allow_nan** (bool) - Optional - Allow NaN and Infinity values. Defaults to True.
- **default** (Callable) - Optional - A callable that converts values that cannot be encoded into something that can be JSON encoded. Defaults to None.
- **sort_keys** (bool) - Optional - Sort dictionary keys. Defaults to False.

### Example
```python
from rich.json import JSON
json_renderable = JSON('{"key": "value"}')
```
```

### Console.log

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Logs rich content to the terminal with configurable separators, styles, and highlighting.

```APIDOC
## Console.log(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', style=None, justify=None, emoji=None, markup=None, highlight=None, log_locals=False, _stack_offset=1)

### Description
Log rich content to the terminal.

### Parameters
- **objects** (positional args) - Required - Objects to log to the terminal.
- **sep** (str, optional) - Optional - String to write between print data. Defaults to " ".
- **end** (str, optional) - Optional - String to write at end of print data. Defaults to "\n".
- **style** (Union[str, Style], optional) - Optional - A style to apply to output. Defaults to None.
- **justify** (str, optional) - Optional - One of "left", "right", "center", or "full". Defaults to `None`.
- **emoji** (Optional[bool], optional) - Optional - Enable emoji code, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to None.
- **markup** (Optional[bool], optional) - Optional - Enable markup, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to None.
- **highlight** (Optional[bool], optional) - Optional - Enable automatic highlighting, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to None.
- **log_locals** (bool, optional) - Optional - Boolean to enable logging of locals where `log()` was called. Defaults to False.
- **_stack_offset** (int, optional) - Optional - Offset of caller from end of call stack. Defaults to 1.

### Return Type
None
```

--------------------------------

### add_task(description, start=True, total=100.0, completed=0, visible=True, **fields)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Adds a new task to the Progress display. Returns a TaskID that can be used to update the task later.

```APIDOC
## add_task(description, start=True, total=100.0, completed=0, visible=True, **fields)

### Description
Add a new 'task' to the Progress display.

### Method
add_task

### Parameters
- **description** (str) - Required - A description of the task.
- **start** (bool, optional) - Start the task immediately (to calculate elapsed time). If set to False, you will need to call start manually. Defaults to True.
- **total** (float, optional) - Number of total steps in the progress if known. Set to None to render a pulsing animation. Defaults to 100.
- **completed** (int, optional) - Number of steps completed so far. Defaults to 0.
- **visible** (bool, optional) - Enable display of the task. Defaults to True.
- **fields** (str) - Additional data fields required for rendering.

### Returns
An ID you can use when calling update.

### Return Type
TaskID
```

### Theme Class

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/theme.rst

Documentation for the Theme class and its methods.

```APIDOC
## Theme

### Description
A container for style information, used by Console.

### Constructor
`Theme(styles=None, inherit=True)`

### Parameters
- **styles** (Dict[str, Style], optional) - A mapping of style names on to styles. Defaults to None for a theme with no styles.
- **inherit** (bool, optional) - Inherit default styles. Defaults to True.

### Properties
- **config** (str) - Get contents of a config file for this theme.

### Class Methods
- **from_file(config_file, source=None, inherit=True)** - Load a theme from a text mode file.
  - **Parameters:**
    - **config_file** (IO[str]) - An open conf file.
    - **source** (str, optional) - The filename of the open file. Defaults to None.
    - **inherit** (bool, optional) - Inherit default styles. Defaults to True.
  - **Returns:** A new theme instance.
  - **Return type:** Theme

- **read(path, inherit=True, encoding=None)** - Read a theme from a path.
  - **Parameters:**
    - **path** (str) - Path to a config file readable by Python configparser module.
    - **inherit** (bool, optional) - Inherit default styles. Defaults to True.
    - **encoding** (str, optional) - Encoding of the config file. Defaults to None.
  - **Returns:** A new theme instance.
  - **Return type:** Theme
```

    title="Star Wars Movies"
)

Creates a grid-style table with no lines, headers, or footer, with configurable padding and expansion.

```APIDOC
## grid(\*headers, padding=0, collapse_padding=True, pad_edge=False, expand=False) (classmethod)

### Description
Get a table with no lines, headers, or footer.

### Parameters
- **\*headers** (Union[Column, str]) - Column headers, either as a string, or Column instance.
- **padding** (PaddingDimensions, optional) - Get padding around cells. Defaults to 0.
- **collapse_padding** (bool, optional) - Enable collapsing of padding around cells. Defaults to True.
- **pad_edge** (bool, optional) - Enable padding around edges of table. Defaults to False.
- **expand** (bool, optional) - Expand the table to fit the available space if True, otherwise the table width will be auto-calculated. Defaults to False.

### Progress

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Renders an auto-updating progress bar(s). This class is the main entry point for creating progress displays.

```APIDOC
## Progress

### Description
Renders an auto-updating progress bar(s).

### Constructor Parameters
- **console** (Console, optional) - Optional Console instance. Defaults to an internal Console instance writing to stdout.
- **auto_refresh** (bool, optional) - Enable auto refresh. If disabled, you will need to call refresh().
- **refresh_per_second** (float, optional) - Number of times per second to refresh the progress information. Defaults to 10.
- **speed_estimate_period** (float) - Period (in seconds) used to calculate the speed estimate. Defaults to 30.
- **transient** (bool) - Clear the progress on exit. Defaults to False.
- **redirect_stdout** (bool) - Enable redirection of stdout, so `print` may be used. Defaults to True.
- **redirect_stderr** (bool) - Enable redirection of stderr. Defaults to True.
- **get_time** (Optional[GetTimeCallable]) - A callable that gets the current time, or None to use Console.get_time. Defaults to None.
- **disable** (bool, optional) - Disable progress display. Defaults to False.
- **expand** (bool, optional) - Expand tasks table to fit width. Defaults to False.
- **columns** (Union[str, ProgressColumn]) - Columns to display in the progress bar.

```

### Console.out

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Low-level method to write to the terminal without pretty printing, wrapping, or markup.

```APIDOC
## Console.out

### Description
Output to the terminal. This is a low-level way of writing to the terminal which unlike `print()` won't pretty print, wrap text, or apply markup, but will optionally apply highlighting and a basic style.

### Method
out

### Signature
`out(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', style=None, highlight=None)`

### Parameters
- **objects** (*Any*) - Required - Objects to output.
- **sep** (*str*, optional) - String to write between print data. Defaults to " ".
- **end** (*str*, optional) - String to write at end of print data. Defaults to "\n".
- **style** (*Union[str, Style]*, optional) - A style to apply to output. Defaults to None.
- **highlight** (*Optional[bool]*, optional) - Enable automatic highlighting, or `None` to use console default. Defaults to `None`.

### Return Type
None
```

## render_lines(renderable, options=None, style=None, pad=True, new_lines=False)

### guess_lexer(path, code=None)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/syntax.rst

Class method to guess the Pygments lexer alias based on a file path and optional code content. It uses a combination of code and filename to determine the best lexer, returning the lexer name as a string.

```APIDOC
## guess_lexer(path, code=None)

### Description
Class method to guess the Pygments lexer alias based on a file path and optional code content. It uses a combination of code and filename to determine the best lexer, returning the lexer name as a string.

### Method
Classmethod

### Parameters
- **path** (AnyStr) - Required - The path to the file containing the code you wish to know the lexer for.
- **code** (str, optional) - Optional - Optional string of code that will be used as a fallback if no lexer is found for the supplied path.

### Returns
- **str** - The name of the Pygments lexer that best matches the supplied path/code.

```

### save_html

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Generates HTML from console contents and writes it to a file. Requires `record=True` argument in constructor.

```APIDOC
## save_html(path, theme=None, clear=True, code_format='<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html>\n<head>\n<meta charset="UTF-8">\n<style>\n{stylesheet}\nbody {{\n    color: {foreground};\n    background-color: {background};\n}}\n</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n    <pre style="font-family:Menlo,\'DejaVu Sans Mono\',consolas,\'Courier New\',monospace"><code style="font-family:inherit">{code}</code></pre>\n</body>\n</html>\n', inline_styles=False)

### Description
Generates HTML from console contents and writes it to a file. Requires `record=True` argument in constructor.

### Parameters
- **path** (Union[str, PathLike[str]]) - Required - Path to write html file.
- **theme** (TerminalTheme, optional) - TerminalTheme object containing console colors.
- **clear** (bool, optional) - Clear record buffer after exporting. Defaults to `True`.
- **code_format** (str, optional) - Format string to render HTML. In addition to '{foreground}', '{background}', and '{code}', should contain '{stylesheet}' if inline_styles is `False`.
- **inline_styles** (bool, optional) - If `True` styles will be inlined in to spans, which makes files larger but easier to cut and paste markup. If `False`, styles will be embedded in a style tag. Defaults to False.

### Returns
- **None** - No return value.
```

### Implement __rich_repr__ with rich.repr.Result

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/pretty.rst

Define a class with a manually implemented __rich_repr__ method that yields positional and keyword arguments. The method is annotated with rich.repr.Result to help catch logical errors. Use this when you need full control over the repr output.

```python
import rich.repr


class Bird:
    def __init__(self, name, eats=None, fly=True, extinct=False):
        self.name = name
        self.eats = list(eats) if eats else []
        self.fly = fly
        self.extinct = extinct

    def __rich_repr__(self) -> rich.repr.Result:
        yield self.name
        yield "eats", self.eats
        yield "fly", self.fly, True
        yield "extinct", self.extinct, False
```

### Capture console output with context manager

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Use the capture context manager to store the output of print or log calls into a string instead of writing directly to the terminal.

```pycon
>>> from rich.console import Console
>>> console = Console()
>>> with console.capture() as capture:
...     console.print("[bold magenta]Hello World[/]")
>>> print(capture.get())
```

### rich.progress.wrap_file

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Function to read bytes from a file while tracking progress.

```APIDOC
## rich.progress.wrap_file(file, total, description='Reading...', auto_refresh=True, console=None, transient=False, get_time=None, refresh_per_second=10, style='bar.back', complete_style='bar.complete', finished_style='bar.finished', pulse_style='bar.pulse', disable=False)

### Description
Read bytes from a file while tracking progress.

### Parameters
- **file** (Union[str, PathLike[str], BinaryIO]) - Required - The path to the file to read, or a file-like object in binary mode.
- **total** (int) - Required - Total number of bytes to read.
- **description** (str, optional) - Description of task show next to progress bar. Defaults to "Reading".
- **auto_refresh** (bool, optional) - Automatic refresh, disable to force a refresh after each iteration. Default is True.
- **transient** (bool) - Clear the progress on exit. Defaults to False.
- **console** (Console, optional) - Console to write to. Default creates internal Console instance.
- **refresh_per_second** (float) - Number of times per second to refresh the progress information. Defaults to 10.
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for the bar background. Defaults to "bar.back".
- **complete_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for the completed bar. Defaults to "bar.complete".
- **finished_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for a finished bar. Defaults to "bar.finished".
- **pulse_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for pulsing bars. Defaults to "bar.pulse".
- **disable** (bool, optional) - Disable display of progress.
- **get_time** (Optional[Callable[[], float]]) - Optional callable to get the current time.

### Returns
A context manager yielding a progress reader.

### Return Type
ContextManager[BinaryIO]
```

### Align(renderable, align='left', style=None, vertical=None, pad=True, width=None, height=None)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/align.rst

Align a renderable by adding spaces if necessary. Supports horizontal alignment (left, center, right) and optional vertical alignment.

```APIDOC
## Align(renderable, align='left', style=None, vertical=None, pad=True, width=None, height=None)

### Description
Align a renderable by adding spaces if necessary. Supports horizontal alignment (left, center, right) and optional vertical alignment.

### Constructor Parameters
- **renderable** (RenderableType) - Required - A console renderable.
- **align** (AlignMethod) - Optional - One of "left", "center", or "right". Defaults to "left".
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Optional - An optional style to apply to the background.
- **vertical** (Optional[VerticalAlignMethod], optional) - Optional - Optional vertical align, one of "top", "middle", or "bottom". Defaults to None.
- **pad** (bool, optional) - Optional - Pad the right with spaces. Defaults to True.
- **width** (int, optional) - Optional - Restrict contents to given width, or None to use default width. Defaults to None.
- **height** (int, optional) - Optional - Set height of align renderable, or None to fit to contents. Defaults to None.

### Raises
- **ValueError** - if `align` is not one of the expected values.

### Example
```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.align import Align
from rich.panel import Panel

console = Console()
# Create a panel 20 characters wide
p = Panel("Hello, [b]World[/b]!", style="on green", width=20)

# Renders the panel centered in the terminal
console.print(Align(p, align="center"))
```
```

--------------------------------

### Progress.reset()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference.rst

Resets a task to its initial state.

```APIDOC
## Progress.reset()

### Description
Resets a task to its initial state.

### Method
reset

### Parameters
- **task_id** (TaskID) - Required - The ID of the task.
- **total** (float, optional) - Optional - New total.
- **completed** (float, optional) - Optional - New completed value.

### Returns
None
```

### Page output with console.pager()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Use the pager context manager to display long output. The pager command is determined by the MANPAGER or PAGER environment variables, with MANPAGER taking priority. Color is stripped by default; set styles=True to preserve ANSI styles if the pager supports them.

```python
from rich.__main__ import make_test_card
from rich.console import Console

console = Console()
with console.pager():
    console.print(make_test_card())
```

--------------------------------

### print_exception

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Prints a rich render of the last exception and traceback.

```APIDOC
## print_exception(width=100, extra_lines=3, theme=None, word_wrap=False, show_locals=False, suppress=(), max_frames=100)

### Description
Prints a rich render of the last exception and traceback.

### Parameters
- **width** (Optional[int], optional) - Number of characters used to render code. Defaults to 100.
- **extra_lines** (int, optional) - Additional lines of code to render. Defaults to 3.
- **theme** (str, optional) - Override pygments theme used in traceback.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) - Enable word wrapping of long lines. Defaults to False.
- **show_locals** (bool, optional) - Enable display of local variables. Defaults to False.
- **suppress** (Iterable[Union[str, ModuleType]]) - Optional sequence of modules or paths to exclude from traceback.
- **max_frames** (int) - Maximum number of frames to show in a traceback, 0 for no maximum. Defaults to 100.

### Returns
None
```

### Confirm.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

Confirm is a yes/no confirmation prompt. Its ask method returns a boolean result.

```APIDOC
## Confirm.ask

### Description
A yes / no confirmation prompt. The ask method is inherited from PromptBase and returns a boolean result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop. For Confirm, this is a boolean.

### Example
```pycon
>>> if Confirm.ask("Continue"):
        run_job()
```
```

### Split layout into panels with Layout.split

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/layout.rst

Divides the 'right' layout into two panels using the split method. The first positional argument to Layout can be any Rich renderable, which will be sized to fit within the layout's area.

```python
from rich.panel import Panel

layout["right"].split(
    Layout(Panel("Hello")),
    Layout(Panel("World!"))
)

### track(sequence, total=None, completed=0, task_id=None, description='Working...', update_period=0.1)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Track progress by iterating over a sequence. You can also track progress of an iterable, which might require that you additionally specify `total`.

```APIDOC
## track(sequence, total=None, completed=0, task_id=None, description='Working...', update_period=0.1)

### Description
Track progress by iterating over a sequence. You can also track progress of an iterable, which might require that you additionally specify `total`.

### Parameters
- **sequence** (Iterable[ProgressType]) - Required - Values you want to iterate over and track progress.
- **total** (float | None) - Optional - Total number of steps. Default is len(sequence).
- **completed** (int, optional) - Number of steps completed so far. Defaults to 0.
- **task_id** (TaskID | None) - Optional - Task to track. Default is new task.
- **description** (str) - Optional - Description of task, if new task is created.
- **update_period** (float, optional) - Minimum time (in seconds) between calls to update(). Defaults to 0.1.

### Returns
Iterable[ProgressType] - An iterable of values taken from the provided sequence.
```

### Rich API Reference Index

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference.rst

An index of the Rich library's public API, listing classes and their methods for styled text, tables, syntax highlighting, and more.

```APIDOC
## Rich API Reference

### Description
This is a reference index for the Rich library, providing links to detailed documentation for each class and method.

### Classes and Methods

#### rich.styled
- **Styled** - A class for applying styles to content.

#### rich.syntax
- **Syntax** - A class for syntax highlighting.
  - **Syntax.default_lexer** - The default lexer used for syntax highlighting.
  - **Syntax.from_path()** - Create a Syntax object from a file path.
  - **Syntax.get_theme()** - Get the syntax highlighting theme.
  - **Syntax.guess_lexer()** - Guess the lexer for a given code string.
  - **Syntax.highlight()** - Highlight the code with syntax highlighting.
  - **Syntax.lexer** - The lexer used for syntax highlighting.
  - **Syntax.stylize_range()** - Apply styles to a range of text.

#### rich.table
- **Column** - A class representing a table column.
  - **Column.cells** - The cells in the column.
  - **Column.copy()** - Copy the column.
  - **Column.flexible** - Whether the column is flexible.
  - **Column.footer** - The footer text.
  - **Column.footer_style** - The style for the footer.
  - **Column.header** - The header text.
  - **Column.header_style** - The style for the header.
  - **Column.highlight** - Whether to highlight the column.
  - **Column.justify** - The justification of the column.
  - **Column.max_width** - The maximum width of the column.
  - **Column.min_width** - The minimum width of the column.
  - **Column.no_wrap** - Whether to disable wrapping.
  - **Column.overflow** - The overflow behavior.
  - **Column.ratio** - The ratio of the column.
  - **Column.style** - The style of the column.
  - **Column.vertical** - The vertical alignment.
  - **Column.width** - The width of the column.
- **Row** - A class representing a table row.
  - **Row.end_section** - Whether the row ends a section.
  - **Row.style** - The style of the row.
- **Table** - A class for creating tables.
  - **Table.add_column()** - Add a column to the table.
  - **Table.add_row()** - Add a row to the table.
  - **Table.add_section()** - Add a section to the table.
  - **Table.expand** - Whether the table expands to fill available space.
  - **Table.get_row_style()** - Get the style of a row.
  - **Table.grid()** - Create a grid table.
  - **Table.padding** - The padding of the table.
  - **Table.row_count** - The number of rows in the table.

#### rich.text
- **Text** - A class for styled text.
  - **Text.align()** - Align the text.
  - **Text.append()** - Append text.
  - **Text.append_text()** - Append another Text object.
  - **Text.append_tokens()** - Append tokens.
  - **Text.apply_meta()** - Apply metadata to the text.
  - **Text.assemble()** - Assemble text from parts.
  - **Text.blank_copy()** - Create a blank copy of the text.
  - **Text.cell_len** - The cell length of the text.
  - **Text.copy()** - Copy the text.
  - **Text.copy_styles()** - Copy styles from another text.
  - **Text.detect_indentation()** - Detect the indentation of the text.
  - **Text.divide()** - Divide the text into parts.
  - **Text.expand_tabs()** - Expand tabs in the text.
  - **Text.extend_style()** - Extend the style of the text.
  - **Text.fit()** - Fit the text to a width.
  - **Text.from_ansi()** - Create a Text object from ANSI codes.
  - **Text.from_markup()** - Create a Text object from markup.
  - **Text.get_style_at_offset()** - Get the style at a given offset.
  - **Text.highlight_regex()** - Highlight regex matches.
  - **Text.highlight_words()** - Highlight words.
  - **Text.join()** - Join text with a separator.
  - **Text.markup** - The markup of the text.
  - **Text.on()** - Apply a style to the text.
  - **Text.pad()** - Pad the text.
  - **Text.pad_left()** - Pad the left side of the text.
  - **Text.pad_right()** - Pad the right side of the text.
  - **Text.plain** - The plain text content.
```

--------------------------------

### Rich output with nested formatting

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/pretty.rst

The output remains readable even with less room in the terminal or deeply nested data structures.

```default
{
    'gull': Bird(
        'gull',
        eats=[
            'fish',
            'chips',
            'ice cream',
            'sausage rolls'
        ]
    ),
    'penguin': Bird(
        'penguin',
        eats=['fish'],
        fly=False
    ),
    'dodo': Bird(
        'dodo',
        eats=['fruit'],
        fly=False,
        extinct=True
    )
}
```

--------------------------------

### Render markdown with rich.markdown.Markdown

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Render markdown content to the terminal. It reads a file (README.md) and constructs a Markdown object, then prints it to the console.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.markdown import Markdown

console = Console()
with open("README.md") as readme:
    markdown = Markdown(readme.read())
console.print(markdown)
```

### Print traceback with print_exception

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/traceback.rst

Use Console.print_exception() to print a traceback for the current exception. The show_locals=True parameter displays the value of local variables for each frame.

```default
from rich.console import Console
console = Console()

try:
    do_something()
except Exception:
    console.print_exception(show_locals=True)

## Segment.split_and_crop_lines(segments, length, style=None, pad=True, include_new_lines=True)

### Layout.split

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/layout.rst

Splits the layout into multiple sub-layouts. The splitter can be specified as a Splitter instance or a string name.

```APIDOC
## Layout.split

### Description
Splits the layout into multiple sub-layouts. The splitter can be specified as a Splitter instance or a string name.

### Method
split

### Signature
split(*layouts, splitter='column')

### Parameters
- **\*layouts** (Layout) - Positional arguments should be (sub) Layout instances.
- **splitter** (Union[Splitter, str]) - Splitter instance or name of splitter.

### Returns
None

```

### print_json

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Pretty prints JSON. Output will be valid JSON.

```APIDOC
## print_json(json=None, data=None, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

### Description
Pretty prints JSON. Output will be valid JSON.

### Parameters
- **json** (Optional[str]) - A string containing JSON.
- **data** (Any) - If json is not supplied, then encode this data.
- **indent** (Union[None, int, str], optional) - Number of spaces to indent. Defaults to 2.
- **highlight** (bool, optional) - Enable highlighting of output. Defaults to True.
- **skip_keys** (bool, optional) - Skip keys not of a basic type. Defaults to False.
- **ensure_ascii** (bool, optional) - Escape all non-ascii characters. Defaults to False.
- **check_circular** (bool, optional) - Check for circular references. Defaults to True.
- **allow_nan** (bool, optional) - Allow NaN and Infinity values. Defaults to True.
- **default** (Callable, optional) - A callable that converts values that can not be encoded in to something that can be JSON encoded. Defaults to None.
- **sort_keys** (bool, optional) - Sort dictionary keys. Defaults to False.

### Returns
None
```

### Track progress with rich.progress.track

Basic usage of the track function to display a progress bar while iterating over a sequence. Wrap any sequence in track and iterate over the result.

from rich.progress import track

for step in track(range(100)):

### Console.pager

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Context manager to display printed content within a system-defined pager.

```APIDOC
## Console.pager

### Description
A context manager to display anything printed within a "pager". The pager application is defined by the system and will typically support at least pressing a key to scroll.

### Method
pager

### Signature
`pager(pager=None, styles=False, links=False)`

### Parameters
- **pager** (*Pager*, optional) - A pager object, or None to use `SystemPager`. Defaults to None.
- **styles** (*bool*, optional) - Show styles in pager. Defaults to False.
- **links** (*bool*, optional) - Show links in pager. Defaults to False.

### Returns
A context manager.

### Return Type
PagerContext

### Example
```pycon
>>> from rich.console import Console
>>> from rich.__main__ import make_test_card
>>> console = Console()
>>> with console.pager():
        console.print(make_test_card())
```
```

### Rich output with standard repr

{
    'gull': Bird('gull', eats=['fish', 'chips', 'ice cream', 'sausage rolls'],
fly=True, extinct=False),
    'penguin': Bird('penguin', eats=['fish'], fly=False, extinct=False),

### from_data(data, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/json.rst

Class method that encodes a JSON object from arbitrary data and returns a new JSON renderable.

```APIDOC
## from_data(data, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

### Description
Class method that encodes a JSON object from arbitrary data.

### Parameters
- **data** (Any) - Required - An object that may be encoded into JSON.
- **indent** (Union[None, int, str]) - Optional - Number of characters to indent by. Defaults to 2.
- **highlight** (bool) - Optional - Enable highlighting. Defaults to True.
- **skip_keys** (bool) - Optional - Skip keys not of a basic type. Defaults to False.
- **ensure_ascii** (bool) - Optional - Escape all non-ascii characters. Defaults to False.
- **check_circular** (bool) - Optional - Check for circular references. Defaults to True.
- **allow_nan** (bool) - Optional - Allow NaN and Infinity values. Defaults to True.
- **default** (Callable) - Optional - A callable that converts values that cannot be encoded into something that can be JSON encoded. Defaults to None.
- **sort_keys** (bool) - Optional - Sort dictionary keys. Defaults to False.

### Returns
- **JSON** - New JSON object from the given data.

### Example
```python
from rich.json import JSON
json_renderable = JSON.from_data({'key': 'value'})
```
```

### Add __rich_repr__ method

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/pretty.rst

Add this __rich_repr__ method to the Bird class to enable Rich formatting. It yields tuples that specify positional and keyword arguments, with defaults omitted.

```default
def __rich_repr__(self):
    yield self.name
    yield "eats", self.eats
    yield "fly", self.fly, True
    yield "extinct", self.extinct, False
```

--------------------------------

log.error("[bold red blink]Server is shutting down![/]", extra={"markup": True})

### Use Rich markup for inline styling

Uses Rich's markup syntax, similar to BBCode, for finely grained styling within a string.

```python
console.print("Where there is a [bold cyan]Will[/bold cyan] there [u]is[/u] a [i]way[/i].")
```

### Use rich print for styled output

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Imports the rich print method, which has the same signature as the builtin print function, and uses it to print styled text with markup and emoji.

```python
from rich import print

print("Hello, [bold magenta]World[/bold magenta]!", ":vampire:", locals())
```

### rich.print()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/init.rst

Prints objects to the terminal with rich formatting, mirroring the built-in print signature.

```APIDOC
## rich.print(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', file=None, flush=False)

### Description
Print object(s) supplied via positional arguments. This function has an identical signature to the built-in print. For more advanced features, see the Console class.

### Method
Function

### Signature
`rich.print(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', file=None, flush=False)`

### Parameters
- **objects** (Any) - Required - Objects to print.
- **sep** (str, optional) - Separator between printed objects. Defaults to " ".
- **end** (str, optional) - Character to write at end of output. Defaults to "\n".
- **file** (IO[str], optional) - File to write to, or None for stdout. Defaults to None.
- **flush** (bool, optional) - Has no effect as Rich always flushes output. Defaults to False.

### Returns
- **None**
```

New current style (also available as stack.current)

### rich.text.Text

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/text.rst

Documentation for the rich.text.Text class and its methods.

```APIDOC
## Text Class

### Description
Represents text with color and style, providing methods for alignment, appending, metadata, and more.

### Constructor
`Text(text='', style='', justify=None, overflow=None, no_wrap=None, end='\n', tab_size=None, spans=None)`

#### Parameters
- **text** (str, optional) - Default unstyled text. Defaults to "".
- **style** (Union[str, Style], optional) - Base style for text. Defaults to "".
- **justify** (str, optional) - Justify method: "left", "center", "full", "right". Defaults to None.
- **overflow** (str, optional) - Overflow method: "crop", "fold", "ellipsis". Defaults to None.
- **no_wrap** (bool, optional) - Disable text wrapping, or None for default. Defaults to None.
- **end** (str, optional) - Character to end text with. Defaults to "\n".
- **tab_size** (int) - Number of spaces per tab, or `None` to use `console.tab_size`. Defaults to None.
- **spans** (List[Span], optional) - List of spans.

### Methods

#### align(align, width, character=' ')
Align text to a given width.

- **Parameters:**
  - **align** (AlignMethod) - One of "left", "center", or "right".
  - **width** (int) - Desired width.
  - **character** (str, optional) - Character to pad with. Defaults to " ".
- **Return type:** None

#### append(text, style=None)
Add text with an optional style.

- **Parameters:**
  - **text** (Union[Text, str]) - A str or Text to append.
  - **style** (str, optional) - A style name. Defaults to None.
- **Returns:** Returns self for chaining.
- **Return type:** Text

#### append_text(text)
Append another Text instance. This method is more performant than Text.append, but only works for Text.

- **Parameters:**
  - **text** (Text) - The Text instance to append to this instance.
- **Returns:** Returns self for chaining.
- **Return type:** Text

#### append_tokens(tokens)
Append iterable of str and style. Style may be a Style instance or a str style definition.

- **Parameters:**
  - **tokens** (Iterable[Tuple[str, Optional[StyleType]]]) - An iterable of tuples containing str content and style.
- **Returns:** Returns self for chaining.
- **Return type:** Text

#### apply_meta(meta, start=0, end=None)
Apply metadata to the text, or a portion of the text.

- **Parameters:**
  - **meta** (Dict[str, Any]) - A dict of meta information.
  - **start** (int) - Start offset (negative indexing is supported). Defaults to 0.
  - **end** (Optional[int], optional) - End offset (negative indexing is supported), or None for end of text. Defaults to None.
- **Return type:** None

#### assemble(\*parts, style='', justify=None, overflow=None, no_wrap=None, end='\n', tab_size=8, meta=None)
Construct a text instance by combining a sequence of strings with optional styles. The positional arguments should be either strings, or a tuple of string + style.

- **Parameters:**
  - **style** (Union[str, Style], optional) - Base style for text. Defaults to "".
  - **justify** (str, optional) - Justify method: "left", "center", "full", "right". Defaults to None.
  - **overflow** (str, optional) - Overflow method: "crop", "fold", "ellipsis". Defaults to None.
  - **no_wrap** (bool, optional) - Disable text wrapping, or None for default. Defaults to None.
  - **end** (str, optional) - Character to end text with. Defaults to "\n".
  - **tab_size** (int) - Number of spaces per tab, or `None` to use `console.tab_size`. Defaults to None.
  - **meta** (Dict[str, Any], optional) - Metadata.
  - **parts** (str | Text | Tuple[str, str | Style]) - Parts to assemble.
- **Returns:** A new text instance.
- **Return type:** Text

#### blank_copy(plain='')
Return a new Text instance with copied metadata (but not the string or spans).

- **Parameters:**
  - **plain** (str) - Plain text.
- **Return type:** Text

#### cell_len
Get the number of cells required to render this text.

- **Return type:** int

#### copy()
Return a copy of this instance.

- **Return type:** Text

#### copy_styles(text)
Copy styles from another Text instance.

- **Parameters:**
  - **text** (Text) - A Text instance to copy styles from, must be the same length.
- **Return type:** None

#### detect_indentation()
Auto-detect indentation of code.

- **Returns:** Number of spaces used to indent code.
- **Return type:** int

#### divide(offsets)
Divide text into a number of lines at given offsets.

- **Parameters:**
  - **offsets** (Iterable[int]) - Offsets used to divide text.
- **Returns:** New RichText instances between offsets.
- **Return type:** Lines
```

### rich.pretty.Pretty

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/pretty.rst

A rich renderable that pretty prints an object. It accepts various formatting options to control indentation, wrapping, truncation, and expansion.

```APIDOC
## rich.pretty.Pretty

### Description
A rich renderable that pretty prints an object.

### Constructor Parameters
- **_object** (Any) - Required - An object to pretty print.
- **highlighter** (HighlighterType, optional) - Highlighter object to apply to result, or None for ReprHighlighter. Defaults to None.
- **indent_size** (int, optional) - Number of spaces in indent. Defaults to 4.
- **justify** (JustifyMethod, optional) - Justify method, or None for default. Defaults to None.
- **overflow** (OverflowMethod, optional) - Overflow method, or None for default. Defaults to None.
- **no_wrap** (Optional[bool], optional) - Disable word wrapping. Defaults to False.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) - Enable indentation guides. Defaults to False.
- **max_length** (int, optional) - Maximum length of containers before abbreviating, or None for no abbreviation. Defaults to None.
- **max_string** (int, optional) - Maximum length of string before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **max_depth** (int, optional) - Maximum depth of nested data structures, or None for no maximum. Defaults to None.
- **expand_all** (bool, optional) - Expand all containers. Defaults to False.
- **margin** (int, optional) - Subtract a margin from width to force containers to expand earlier. Defaults to 0.
- **insert_line** (bool, optional) - Insert a new line if the output has multiple new lines. Defaults to False.
```

### Column

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/table.rst

Defines a column within a Table. The constructor accepts various parameters to control the column's header, footer, style, alignment, width, and other display properties.

```APIDOC
## Column

### Description
Defines a column within a Table. The constructor accepts various parameters to control the column's header, footer, style, alignment, width, and other display properties.

### Constructor Parameters
- **header** (RenderableType) - Required - Renderable for the header (typically a string).
- **footer** (RenderableType) - Required - Renderable for the footer (typically a string).
- **header_style** (StyleType) - Optional - The style of the header. Defaults to ''.
- **footer_style** (StyleType) - Optional - The style of the footer. Defaults to ''.
- **style** (StyleType) - Optional - Default style for the column. Defaults to ''.
- **justify** (JustifyMethod) - Optional - How to justify text within the column ("left", "center", "right", or "full"). Defaults to 'left'.
- **vertical** (VerticalAlignMethod) - Optional - Vertical alignment of the column content. Defaults to 'top'.
- **overflow** (OverflowMethod) - Optional - Overflow handling for the column. Defaults to 'ellipsis'.
- **width** (int) - Optional - The width in characters of the column, or None to automatically fit. Defaults to None.
- **min_width** (int) - Optional - The minimum width of the column, or None for no minimum. Defaults to None.
- **max_width** (int) - Optional - Maximum width of column, or None for no maximum. Defaults to None.
- **ratio** (int) - Optional - The ratio of the column width relative to other columns. Defaults to None.
- **no_wrap** (bool) - Optional - Disable wrapping of text within the column. Defaults to False.
- **highlight** (bool) - Optional - Apply highlighter to column. Defaults to False.
- **_index** (int) - Optional - Internal index of the column. Defaults to 0.
- **_cells** (List[RenderableType]) - Optional - Internal list of cells in the column. Defaults to an empty list.

### Properties
- **cells** (Iterable[RenderableType]) - Get all cells in the column, not including header.
- **flexible** (bool) - Check if this column is flexible.
- **footer** (RenderableType) - Renderable for the footer (typically a string).
- **footer_style** (StyleType) - The style of the footer.
- **header** (RenderableType) - Renderable for the header (typically a string).
- **header_style** (StyleType) - The style of the header.
- **highlight** (bool) - Apply highlighter to column. Defaults to False.
- **justify** (JustifyMethod) - How to justify text within the column ("left", "center", "right", or "full").
- **max_width** (int) - Maximum width of column, or None for no maximum.

### Methods
#### copy()
Return a copy of this Column.

**Return type:** Column

```

### Status

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/status.rst

The Status class displays a status indicator with a 'spinner' animation. It is initialized with a status renderable and optional console, spinner, style, speed, and refresh rate parameters.

```APIDOC
## Status

### Description
Displays a status indicator with a 'spinner' animation.

### Constructor Parameters
- **status** (RenderableType) - Required - A status renderable (str or Text typically).
- **console** (Console, optional) - Console instance to use, or None for global console. Defaults to None.
- **spinner** (str, optional) - Name of spinner animation (see python -m rich.spinner). Defaults to "dots".
- **spinner_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style of spinner. Defaults to "status.spinner".
- **speed** (float, optional) - Speed factor for spinner animation. Defaults to 1.0.
- **refresh_per_second** (float, optional) - Number of refreshes per second. Defaults to 12.5.

### Properties
- **console** (Console) - Get the Console used by the Status objects.
```

### Split lower layout into rows

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/layout.rst

Splits the 'lower' sub-layout into a row of two sub-layouts, resulting in three portions: an upper half and a lower half split into two quarters.

```default
layout["lower"].split_row(
    Layout(name="left"),
    Layout(name="right"),
)
print(layout)
```

--------------------------------

### Export SVG with MONOKAI theme

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Export console output to an SVG file with a custom theme. Import the desired theme from rich.terminal_theme and pass it to save_svg() via the theme parameter. The SVG width matches the terminal width in characters, and the height scales automatically.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.terminal_theme import MONOKAI

console = Console(record=True)
console.save_svg("example.svg", theme=MONOKAI)
```

>>> name = Prompt.ask("Enter your name", choices=["Paul", "Jessica", "Duncan"], default="Paul", case_sensitive=False)

```APIDOC
## rich.table.Column

### Description
Configuration options for a table column, including width, wrapping, overflow, alignment, and style.

### Attributes

#### min_width (Optional[int]) - Default: None
Minimum width of column, or `None` for no minimum.

#### no_wrap (bool) - Default: False
Prevent wrapping of text within the column.

#### overflow (str) - Default: 'ellipsis'
Overflow method.

#### ratio (Optional[int]) - Default: None
Ratio to use when calculating column width, or `None` to adapt to column contents.

#### style (StyleType) - Default: ''
The style of the column.

#### vertical (str) - Default: 'top'
How to vertically align content ("top", "middle", or "bottom").

#### width (Optional[int]) - Default: None
Width of the column, or `None` to auto calculate width.

--------------------------------

### Nested progress bars with track

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/progress.rst

Creates an outer progress bar for a range of 10 and an inner transient progress bar for each letter in 'ABCDEF'. The inner bar appears below the outer bar and both update, with the nested bars updating according to the outer bar's refresh_per_second attribute.

```python
from rich.progress import track
from time import sleep


for count in track(range(10)):
    for letter in track("ABCDEF", transient=True):
        print(f"Stage {count}{letter}")
        sleep(0.1)
    sleep(0.1)

```

### rich.print_json()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/init.rst

Pretty prints JSON data or a JSON string with syntax highlighting and configurable formatting.

```APIDOC
## rich.print_json(json=None, data=None, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)

### Description
Pretty prints JSON. Output will be valid JSON.

### Method
Function

### Signature
`rich.print_json(json=None, data=None, indent=2, highlight=True, skip_keys=False, ensure_ascii=False, check_circular=True, allow_nan=True, default=None, sort_keys=False)`

### Parameters
- **json** (str) - Optional - A string containing JSON.
- **data** (Any) - Optional - If json is not supplied, then encode this data.
- **indent** (int, optional) - Number of spaces to indent. Defaults to 2.
- **highlight** (bool, optional) - Enable highlighting of output. Defaults to True.
- **skip_keys** (bool, optional) - Skip keys not of a basic type. Defaults to False.
- **ensure_ascii** (bool, optional) - Escape all non-ascii characters. Defaults to False.
- **check_circular** (bool, optional) - Check for circular references. Defaults to True.
- **allow_nan** (bool, optional) - Allow NaN and Infinity values. Defaults to True.
- **default** (Callable, optional) - A callable that converts values that can not be encoded in to something that can be JSON encoded. Defaults to None.
- **sort_keys** (bool, optional) - Sort dictionary keys. Defaults to False.

### Returns
- **None**
```

### Prompt with Console.input()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

```python
from rich.console import Console
console = Console()
console.input("What is [i]your[/i] [bold red]name[/]? :smiley: ")
```

### export_svg

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Exports the console contents as an SVG image. Requires record=True in the Console constructor.

```APIDOC
## export_svg(title='Rich', theme=None, clear=True, code_format='<svg class="rich-terminal" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">\n    <!-- Generated with Rich https://www.textualize.io -->\n    <style>\n\n    @font-face {{\n        font-family: "Fira Code";\n        src: local("FiraCode-Regular"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff2/FiraCode-Regular.woff2") format("woff2"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff/FiraCode-Regular.woff") format("woff");\n        font-style: normal;\n        font-weight: 400;\n    }}\n    @font-face {{\n        font-family: "Fira Code";\n        src: local("FiraCode-Bold"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff2/FiraCode-Bold.woff2") format("woff2"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff/FiraCode-Bold.woff") format("woff");\n        font-style: bold;\n        font-weight: 700;\n    }}\n\n    .{unique_id}-matrix {{\n        font-family: Fira Code, monospace;\n        font-size: {char_height}px;\n        line-height: {line_height}px;\n        font-variant-east-asian: full-width;\n    }}\n\n    .{unique_id}-title {{\n        font-size: 18px;\n        font-weight: bold;\n        font-family: arial;\n    }}\n\n    {styles}\n    </style>\n\n    <defs>\n    <clipPath id="{unique_id}-clip-terminal">\n      <rect x="0" y="0" width="{terminal_width}" height="{terminal_height}" />\n    </clipPath>\n    {lines}\n    </defs>\n\n    {chrome}\n    <g transform="translate({terminal_x}, {terminal_y})" clip-path="url(#{unique_id}-clip-terminal)">\n    {backgrounds}\n    <g class="{unique_id}-matrix">\n    {matrix}\n    </g>\n    </g>\n</svg>\n', font_aspect_ratio=0.61, unique_id=None)

### Description
Generate an SVG from the console contents. Requires record=True in Console constructor.

### Method
Function call

### Endpoint
Console.export_svg()

### Parameters
- **title** (str, optional) - The title of the tab in the output image.
- **theme** (TerminalTheme, optional) - The TerminalTheme object to use to style the terminal.
- **clear** (bool, optional) - Clear record buffer after exporting. Defaults to True.
- **code_format** (str, optional) - Format string used to generate the SVG. Rich will inject a number of variables into the string in order to form the final SVG output. The default template used and the variables injected by Rich can be found by inspecting the console.CONSOLE_SVG_FORMAT variable.
- **font_aspect_ratio** (float, optional) - The width to height ratio of the font used in the code_format string. Defaults to 0.61, which is the width to height ratio of Fira Code (the default font). If you aren't specifying a different font inside code_format, you probably don't need this.
- **unique_id** (str, optional) - unique id that is used as the prefix for various elements (CSS styles, node ids). If not set, this defaults to a computed value based on the recorded content.

### Returns
- **str** - The SVG content as a string.

### Return Type
str
```
