### Traceback.__init__

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/traceback.rst

Constructs a Traceback renderable from a Trace object or the last exception. Accepts numerous options to control width, code display, local variable handling, and suppression.

```APIDOC
## Traceback.__init__

### Description
Constructs a Traceback renderable from a Trace object or the last exception. Accepts numerous options to control width, code display, local variable handling, and suppression.

### Method
Constructor

### Signature
`Traceback(trace=None, *, width=100, code_width=88, extra_lines=3, theme=None, word_wrap=False, show_locals=False, locals_max_length=10, locals_max_string=80, locals_max_depth=None, locals_hide_dunder=True, locals_hide_sunder=False, locals_overlow=None, indent_guides=True, suppress=(), max_frames=100)`

### Parameters
- **trace** (Trace, optional) – A Trace object produced from extract. Defaults to None, which uses the last exception.
- **width** (Optional[int], optional) – Number of characters used to traceback. Defaults to 100.
- **code_width** (Optional[int], optional) – Number of code characters used to traceback. Defaults to 88.
- **extra_lines** (int, optional) – Additional lines of code to render. Defaults to 3.
- **theme** (str, optional) – Override pygments theme used in traceback.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) – Enable word wrapping of long lines. Defaults to False.
- **show_locals** (bool, optional) – Enable display of local variables. Defaults to False.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) – Enable indent guides in code and locals. Defaults to True.
- **locals_max_length** (int, optional) – Maximum length of containers before abbreviating, or None for no abbreviation. Defaults to 10.
- **locals_max_string** (int, optional) – Maximum length of string before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to 80.
- **locals_max_depth** (int, optional) – Maximum depths of locals before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **locals_hide_dunder** (bool, optional) – Hide locals prefixed with double underscore. Defaults to True.
- **locals_hide_sunder** (bool, optional) – Hide locals prefixed with single underscore. Defaults to False.
- **locals_overflow** (OverflowMethod, optional) – How to handle overflowing locals, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **suppress** (Sequence[Union[str, ModuleType]]) – Optional sequence of modules or paths to exclude from traceback.
- **max_frames** (int) – Maximum number of frames to show in a traceback, 0 for no maximum. Defaults to 100.
- **locals_overlow** (Literal['fold', 'crop', 'ellipsis', 'ignore'] | None) – (Note: appears as a parameter in the signature, but no description provided.)

### Returns
A Console renderable that renders a traceback.

### Return Type
Traceback
```

### FloatPrompt.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

FloatPrompt is a prompt that returns a float. Its ask method returns a float result.

```APIDOC
## FloatPrompt.ask

### Description
A prompt that returns a float. The ask method is inherited from PromptBase and returns a float result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop. For FloatPrompt, this is a float.

### Example
```pycon
>>> temperature = FloatPrompt.ask("Enter desired temperature")
```
```

open

### Parameters
- **file** (str | PathLike[str] | bytes) - Required - The path to the file to read.
- **mode** (Literal['rb'] or Literal['r', 'rt']) - Required - The mode to use to open the file. Only supports "r", "rb" or "rt".
- **buffering** (int) - Optional - The buffering strategy to use, see io.open().
- **encoding** (str, optional) - The encoding to use when reading in text mode, see io.open().
- **errors** (str, optional) - The error handling strategy for decoding errors, see io.open().
- **newline** (str, optional) - The strategy for handling newlines in text mode, see io.open().
- **total** (int, optional) - Total number of bytes to read. If none given, os.stat(path).st_size is used.
- **task_id** (TaskID) - Optional - Task to track. Default is new task.
- **description** (str, optional) - Description of task, if new task is created.

### Returns
BinaryIO or TextIO - A readable file-like object.

### Raises
ValueError - When an invalid mode is given.
```

### rich.progress.wrap_file

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Function to read bytes from a file while tracking progress.

```APIDOC
## rich.progress.wrap_file(file, total, description='Reading...', auto_refresh=True, console=None, transient=False, get_time=None, refresh_per_second=10, style='bar.back', complete_style='bar.complete', finished_style='bar.finished', pulse_style='bar.pulse', disable=False)

### Description
Read bytes from a file while tracking progress.

### Parameters
- **file** (Union[str, PathLike[str], BinaryIO]) - Required - The path to the file to read, or a file-like object in binary mode.
- **total** (int) - Required - Total number of bytes to read.
- **description** (str, optional) - Description of task show next to progress bar. Defaults to "Reading".
- **auto_refresh** (bool, optional) - Automatic refresh, disable to force a refresh after each iteration. Default is True.
- **transient** (bool) - Clear the progress on exit. Defaults to False.
- **console** (Console, optional) - Console to write to. Default creates internal Console instance.
- **refresh_per_second** (float) - Number of times per second to refresh the progress information. Defaults to 10.
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for the bar background. Defaults to "bar.back".
- **complete_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for the completed bar. Defaults to "bar.complete".
- **finished_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for a finished bar. Defaults to "bar.finished".
- **pulse_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for pulsing bars. Defaults to "bar.pulse".
- **disable** (bool, optional) - Disable display of progress.
- **get_time** (Optional[Callable[[], float]]) - Optional callable to get the current time.

### Returns
A context manager yielding a progress reader.

### Return Type
ContextManager[BinaryIO]
```

### open

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Documentation for the open function, including parameters and return type.

```APIDOC
## open(file, mode, buffering=-1, encoding=None, errors=None, newline=None, total=None, description='Reading...', auto_refresh=True, console=None, transient=False, get_time=None, refresh_per_second=10, style='bar.back', complete_style='bar.complete', finished_style='bar.finished', pulse_style='bar.pulse', disable=False)

### Description
Opens a file and returns a context manager that provides a TextIO object.

### Parameters
- **file** (str | PathLike[str] | bytes) - The file to open.
- **mode** (Literal['rt', 'r']) - The mode to open the file in.
- **buffering** (int, optional) - Buffering policy. Defaults to -1.
- **encoding** (str | None, optional) - Encoding to use. Defaults to None.
- **errors** (str | None, optional) - Error handling. Defaults to None.
- **newline** (str | None, optional) - Newline control. Defaults to None.
- **total** (int | None, optional) - Total size for progress. Defaults to None.
- **description** (str, optional) - Description for progress. Defaults to 'Reading...'.
- **auto_refresh** (bool, optional) - Auto refresh progress. Defaults to True.
- **console** (Console | None, optional) - Console to use. Defaults to None.
- **transient** (bool, optional) - Transient progress. Defaults to False.
- **get_time** (Callable[[], float] | None, optional) - Time function. Defaults to None.
- **refresh_per_second** (float, optional) - Refresh rate. Defaults to 10.
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for progress bar. Defaults to 'bar.back'.
- **complete_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for completed portion. Defaults to 'bar.complete'.
- **finished_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for finished bar. Defaults to 'bar.finished'.
- **pulse_style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for pulse. Defaults to 'bar.pulse'.
- **disable** (bool, optional) - Disable progress. Defaults to False.

### Returns

```

### Syntax highlight code with rich.syntax.Syntax

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Apply syntax highlighting to a code string using rich.syntax.Syntax. The example uses the monokai theme and enables line numbers, then prints the highlighted code to the console.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.syntax import Syntax

my_code = '''
def iter_first_last(values: Iterable[T]) -> Iterable[Tuple[bool, bool, T]]:
    """Iterate and generate a tuple with a flag for first and last value."""
    iter_values = iter(values)
    try:
        previous_value = next(iter_values)
    except StopIteration:
        return
    first = True
    for value in iter_values:
        yield first, False, previous_value
        first = False
        previous_value = value
    yield first, True, previous_value
'''
syntax = Syntax(my_code, "python", theme="monokai", line_numbers=True)
console = Console()
console.print(syntax)
```

### save_svg

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Method to export console contents to an SVG file.

```APIDOC
## save_svg(path, title='Rich', theme=None, clear=True, code_format='<svg class="rich-terminal" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">\n    <!-- Generated with Rich https://www.textualize.io -->\n    <style>\n\n    @font-face {{\n        font-family: "Fira Code";\n        src: local("FiraCode-Regular"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff2/FiraCode-Regular.woff2") format("woff2"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff/FiraCode-Regular.woff") format("woff");\n        font-style: normal;\n        font-weight: 400;\n    }}\n    @font-face {{\n        font-family: "Fira Code";\n        src: local("FiraCode-Bold"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff2/FiraCode-Bold.woff2") format("woff2"),\n                url("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/firacode/6.2.0/woff/FiraCode-Bold.woff") format("woff");\n        font-style: bold;\n        font-weight: 700;\n    }}\n\n    .{unique_id}-matrix {{\n        font-family: Fira Code, monospace;\n        font-size: {char_height}px;\n        line-height: {line_height}px;\n        font-variant-east-asian: full-width;\n    }}\n\n    .{unique_id}-title {{\n        font-size: 18px;\n        font-weight: bold;\n        font-family: arial;\n    }}\n\n    {styles}\n    </style>\n\n    <defs>\n    <clipPath id="{unique_id}-clip-terminal">\n      <rect x="0" y="0" width="{terminal_width}" height="{terminal_height}" />\n    </clipPath>\n    {lines}\n    </defs>\n\n    {chrome}\n    <g transform="translate({terminal_x}, {terminal_y})" clip-path="url(#{unique_id}-clip-terminal)">\n    {backgrounds}\n    <g class="{unique_id}-matrix">\n    {matrix}\n    </g>\n    </g>\n</svg>\n', font_aspect_ratio=0.61, unique_id=None)

### Description
Generates an SVG file from the console contents. Requires `record=True` in the Console constructor.

### Method
save_svg

### Parameters
- **path** (Union[str, PathLike[str]]) - Required - The path to write the SVG to.
- **title** (str, optional) - The title of the tab in the output image.
- **theme** (TerminalTheme, optional) - The `TerminalTheme` object to use to style the terminal.
- **clear** (bool, optional) - Clear record buffer after exporting. Defaults to `True`.
- **code_format** (str, optional) - Format string used to generate the SVG. Rich will inject a number of variables into the string in order to form the final SVG output. The default template used and the variables injected by Rich can be found by inspecting the `console.CONSOLE_SVG_FORMAT` variable.
- **font_aspect_ratio** (float, optional) - The width to height ratio of the font used in the `code_format` string. Defaults to 0.61, which is the width to height ratio of Fira Code (the default font). If you aren't specifying a different font inside `code_format`, you probably don't need this.
- **unique_id** (str, optional) - Unique id that is used as the prefix for various elements (CSS styles, node ids). If not set, this defaults to a computed value based on the recorded content.

### Return Type
None
```

### guess_lexer(path, code=None)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/syntax.rst

Class method to guess the Pygments lexer alias based on a file path and optional code content. It uses a combination of code and filename to determine the best lexer, returning the lexer name as a string.

```APIDOC
## guess_lexer(path, code=None)

### Description
Class method to guess the Pygments lexer alias based on a file path and optional code content. It uses a combination of code and filename to determine the best lexer, returning the lexer name as a string.

### Method
Classmethod

### Parameters
- **path** (AnyStr) - Required - The path to the file containing the code you wish to know the lexer for.
- **code** (str, optional) - Optional - Optional string of code that will be used as a fallback if no lexer is found for the supplied path.

### Returns
- **str** - The name of the Pygments lexer that best matches the supplied path/code.

```

### Confirm.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

Confirm is a yes/no confirmation prompt. Its ask method returns a boolean result.

```APIDOC
## Confirm.ask

### Description
A yes / no confirmation prompt. The ask method is inherited from PromptBase and returns a boolean result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop. For Confirm, this is a boolean.

### Example
```pycon
>>> if Confirm.ask("Continue"):
        run_job()
```
```

### Traceback.from_exception

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/traceback.rst

Class method to create a Traceback instance from exception information.

```APIDOC
## Traceback.from_exception(exc_type, exc_value, traceback, width=100, code_width=88, extra_lines=3, theme=None, word_wrap=False, show_locals=False, locals_max_length=10, locals_max_string=80, locals_max_depth=None, locals_hide_dunder=True, locals_hide_sunder=False, locals_overflow=None, indent_guides=True, suppress=(), max_frames=100)

### Description
Creates a Traceback instance from exception information. The resulting instance can be printed to display a formatted traceback with syntax highlighting and rich formatting.

### Method
classmethod

### Parameters
- **exc_type** (Type[BaseException]) - Required - Exception type.
- **exc_value** (BaseException) - Required - Exception value.
- **traceback** (TracebackType) - Required - Python Traceback object.
- **width** (Optional[int], optional) - Optional - Number of characters used to traceback. Defaults to 100.
- **code_width** (Optional[int], optional) - Optional - Number of code characters used to traceback. Defaults to 88.
- **extra_lines** (int, optional) - Optional - Additional lines of code to render. Defaults to 3.
- **theme** (str, optional) - Optional - Override pygments theme used in traceback.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable word wrapping of long lines. Defaults to False.
- **show_locals** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable display of local variables. Defaults to False.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) - Optional - Enable indent guides in code and locals. Defaults to True.
- **locals_max_length** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum length of containers before abbreviating, or None for no abbreviation. Defaults to 10.
- **locals_max_depth** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum depths of locals before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **locals_max_string** (int, optional) - Optional - Maximum length of string before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to 80.
- **locals_hide_dunder** (bool, optional) - Optional - Hide locals prefixed with double underscore. Defaults to True.
- **locals_hide_sunder** (bool, optional) - Optional - Hide locals prefixed with single underscore. Defaults to False.
- **locals_overflow** (OverflowMethod, optional) - Optional - How to handle overflowing locals, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **suppress** (Iterable[Union[str, ModuleType]]) - Optional - Optional sequence of modules or paths to exclude from traceback.
- **max_frames** (int) - Optional - Maximum number of frames to show in a traceback, 0 for no maximum. Defaults to 100.

### Returns
A Traceback instance that may be printed.

### Return Type
Traceback
```

### Console.log()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference.rst

Logs a message to the console. This method writes a log message with a timestamp and location to the console.

```APIDOC
## Console.log()

### Description
Logs a message to the console.

### Method
instance method

### Signature
`Console.log(*objects, sep=' ', end='\n', style=None, justify=None, emoji=None, markup=None, highlight=None, log_locals=False, _stack_offset=1)`

### Parameters
- **objects** (Any) - Required - The objects to log.
- **sep** (str, optional) - Optional - Separator between objects.
- **end** (str, optional) - Optional - String appended after the last object.
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Optional - Style to apply.
- **justify** (JustifyMethod, optional) - Optional - Justification method.
- **emoji** (bool, optional) - Optional - Whether to render emoji.
- **markup** (bool, optional) - Optional - Whether to render markup.
- **highlight** (bool, optional) - Optional - Whether to highlight syntax.
- **log_locals** (bool, optional) - Optional - Whether to log local variables.
- **_stack_offset** (int, optional) - Optional - Stack offset for logging.
```

--------------------------------

```APIDOC
## Spinner(name, text='', style=None, speed=1.0)

### Description
Creates a new Spinner instance with the specified name, text, style, and speed. Raises KeyError if the name is not a supported spinner animation.

### Parameters
- **name** (str) - Required - Name of spinner (run python -m rich.spinner).
- **text** (RenderableType, optional) - A renderable to display at the right of the spinner (str or Text typically). Defaults to "".
- **style** (StyleType, optional) - Style for spinner animation. Defaults to None.
- **speed** (float, optional) - Speed factor for animation. Defaults to 1.0.

### Raises
- **KeyError** - If name isn't one of the supported spinner animations.
```

### Render markdown with rich.markdown.Markdown

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Render markdown content to the terminal. It reads a file (README.md) and constructs a Markdown object, then prints it to the console.

```python
from rich.console import Console
from rich.markdown import Markdown

console = Console()
with open("README.md") as readme:
    markdown = Markdown(readme.read())
console.print(markdown)
```

Command to run a demo of the status feature, showing a spinner animation that doesn't interfere with console output.

```default

--------------------------------

### Write console output to a file with file=report_file

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Writes console output to a file by passing a file-like object to the file argument. The example opens report.txt for writing and uses console.rule to print a formatted header. When writing to a file, consider setting the width argument to avoid wrapping to the current console width.

```python
import sys
from rich.console import Console
from datetime import datetime

with open("report.txt", "wt") as report_file:
    console = Console(file=report_file)
    console.rule(f"Report Generated {datetime.now().ctime()}")
```

### Enable console recording with record=True

console = Console(record=True)

### Syntax.__init__

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/syntax.rst

Constructs a Syntax object to render syntax highlighted code. Accepts the code string, a lexer, and various formatting options.

```APIDOC
## Syntax.__init__

### Description
Constructs a Syntax object to render syntax highlighted code.

### Method
Constructor

### Signature
Syntax(code, lexer, theme='monokai', dedent=False, line_numbers=False, start_line=1, line_range=None, highlight_lines=None, code_width=None, tab_size=4, word_wrap=False, background_color=None, indent_guides=False, padding=0)

### Parameters
- **code** (str) - Required - Code to highlight.
- **lexer** (Lexer | str) - Required - Lexer to use (see https://pygments.org/docs/lexers/).
- **theme** (str, optional) - Color theme, aka Pygments style (see https://pygments.org/docs/styles/#getting-a-list-of-available-styles). Defaults to "monokai".
- **dedent** (bool, optional) - Enable stripping of initial whitespace. Defaults to False.
- **line_numbers** (bool, optional) - Enable rendering of line numbers. Defaults to False.
- **start_line** (int, optional) - Starting number for line numbers. Defaults to 1.
- **line_range** (Tuple[int | None, int | None], optional) - If given should be a tuple of the start and end line to render. A value of None in the tuple indicates the range is open in that direction.
- **highlight_lines** (Set[int]) - A set of line numbers to highlight.
- **code_width** (Optional[int]) - Width of code to render (not including line numbers), or None to use all available width.
- **tab_size** (int, optional) - Size of tabs. Defaults to 4.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) - Enable word wrapping.
- **background_color** (str, optional) - Optional background color, or None to use theme color. Defaults to None.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) - Show indent guides. Defaults to False.
- **padding** (PaddingDimensions) - Padding to apply around the syntax. Defaults to 0 (no padding).
```

--------------------------------

### Capture output with StringIO for unit tests

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Alternative capturing method by setting the Console file to an io.StringIO. This is recommended for unit tests; after printing, console.file.getvalue() retrieves the output.

```python
from io import StringIO
from rich.console import Console
console = Console(file=StringIO())
console.print("[bold red]Hello[/] World")
str_output = console.file.getvalue()
```

### Suppress frames with suppress argument

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/traceback.rst

Exclude framework code (e.g., click) from Rich tracebacks by passing a list of modules or str paths to the suppress argument. Suppressed frames show only the line and file.

```default
import click
from rich.traceback import install
install(suppress=[click])
```

--------------------------------

### Prompt.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

The Prompt.ask classmethod is a shortcut to construct and run a prompt loop and return the result.

```APIDOC
## Prompt.ask

### Description
Shortcut to construct and run a prompt loop and return the result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop.

### Example
```pycon
>>> name = Prompt.ask("Enter your name")
```
```

--------------------------------

### Transient progress display

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/progress.rst

Set transient=True on the Progress constructor to remove the progress display from the terminal on exit. Useful for minimal output when tasks complete.

```python
with Progress(transient=True) as progress:
    task = progress.add_task("Working", total=100)
    do_work(task)
```

### Capture console output with context manager

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Use the capture context manager to store the output of print or log calls into a string instead of writing directly to the terminal.

```pycon
>>> from rich.console import Console
>>> console = Console()
>>> with console.capture() as capture:
...     console.print("[bold magenta]Hello World[/]")
>>> print(capture.get())
```

### Console.on_broken_pipe

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Handles BrokenPipeError events, typically by exiting the app; can be overridden in subclasses.

```APIDOC
## Console.on_broken_pipe()

### Description
This function is called when a BrokenPipeError is raised. This can occur when piping Textual output in Linux and macOS. The default implementation is to exit the app, but you could implement this method in a subclass to change the behavior. See https://docs.python.org/3/library/signal.html#note-on-sigpipe for details.

### Return Type
None
```

### add_task(description, start=True, total=100.0, completed=0, visible=True, **fields)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/progress.rst

Adds a new task to the Progress display. Returns a TaskID that can be used to update the task later.

```APIDOC
## add_task(description, start=True, total=100.0, completed=0, visible=True, **fields)

### Description
Add a new 'task' to the Progress display.

### Method
add_task

### Parameters
- **description** (str) - Required - A description of the task.
- **start** (bool, optional) - Start the task immediately (to calculate elapsed time). If set to False, you will need to call start manually. Defaults to True.
- **total** (float, optional) - Number of total steps in the progress if known. Set to None to render a pulsing animation. Defaults to 100.
- **completed** (int, optional) - Number of steps completed so far. Defaults to 0.
- **visible** (bool, optional) - Enable display of the task. Defaults to True.
- **fields** (str) - Additional data fields required for rendering.

### Returns
An ID you can use when calling update.

### Return Type
TaskID
```

### IntPrompt.ask

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/prompt.rst

IntPrompt is a prompt that returns an integer. Its ask method returns an integer result.

```APIDOC
## IntPrompt.ask

### Description
A prompt that returns an integer. The ask method is inherited from PromptBase and returns an integer result.

### Method
ask

### Signature
```python
@classmethod
ask(prompt: str | Text = '', console: Console | None = None, password: bool = False, choices: List[str] | None = None, case_sensitive: bool = True, show_default: bool = True, show_choices: bool = True, default: DefaultType, stream: TextIO | None = None) -> DefaultType | PromptType
```

### Parameters
- **prompt** (str | Text) - Optional - Prompt text. Defaults to "".
- **console** (Console | None) - Optional - A Console instance or None to use global console. Defaults to None.
- **password** (bool) - Optional - Enable password input. Defaults to False.
- **choices** (List[str] | None) - Optional - A list of valid choices. Defaults to None.
- **case_sensitive** (bool) - Optional - Matching of choices should be case-sensitive. Defaults to True.
- **show_default** (bool) - Optional - Show default in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **show_choices** (bool) - Optional - Show choices in prompt. Defaults to True.
- **default** (DefaultType) - Required - Default value to use if no input is provided.
- **stream** (TextIO | None) - Optional - Stream to read input from. Defaults to None.

### Returns
- **DefaultType | PromptType** - The result of the prompt loop. For IntPrompt, this is an integer.

### Example
```pycon
>>> burrito_count = IntPrompt.ask("How many burritos do you want to order")
```
```

### Syntax.from_path

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/syntax.rst

Classmethod that constructs a Syntax object from a file. The lexer can be auto-detected from the path or file content.

```APIDOC
## Syntax.from_path

### Description
Constructs a Syntax object from a file.

### Method
Classmethod

### Signature
from_path(path, encoding='utf-8', lexer=None, theme='monokai', dedent=False, line_numbers=False, line_range=None, start_line=1, highlight_lines=None, code_width=None, tab_size=4, word_wrap=False, background_color=None, indent_guides=False, padding=0)

### Parameters
- **path** (str) - Required - Path to file to highlight.
- **encoding** (str) - Encoding of file. Defaults to 'utf-8'.
- **lexer** (str | Lexer, optional) - Lexer to use. If None, lexer will be auto-detected from path/file content.
- **theme** (str, optional) - Color theme, aka Pygments style (see https://pygments.org/docs/styles/#getting-a-list-of-available-styles). Defaults to "monokai".
- **dedent** (bool, optional) - Enable stripping of initial whitespace. Defaults to True.
- **line_numbers** (bool, optional) - Enable rendering of line numbers. Defaults to False.
- **start_line** (int, optional) - Starting number for line numbers. Defaults to 1.
- **line_range** (Tuple[int, int], optional) - If given should be a tuple of the start and end line to render.
- **highlight_lines** (Set[int]) - A set of line numbers to highlight.
- **code_width** (int | None) - Width of code to render (not including line numbers), or None to use all available width.
- **tab_size** (int, optional) - Size of tabs. Defaults to 4.
- **word_wrap** (bool, optional) - Enable word wrapping of code.
- **background_color** (str, optional) - Optional background color, or None to use theme color. Defaults to None.
- **indent_guides** (bool, optional) - Show indent guides. Defaults to False.
- **padding** (PaddingDimensions) - Padding to apply around the syntax. Defaults to 0 (no padding).

### Returns
- **Syntax** - A Syntax object that may be printed to the console.
```

### Emoji variant example

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/markup.rst

Specify the emoji variant with -emoji or -text.

```pycon
>>> from rich import print
>>> print(":red_heart-emoji:")
>>> print(":red_heart-text:")
```

### Install Rich with pip

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/README.md

Installs the Rich library using pip. Requires Python 3.8 or later.

```sh
python -m pip install rich
```

```

### CodeBlock

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/markdown.rst

A code block with syntax highlighting. The create classmethod is a factory to create markdown elements.

```APIDOC
## CodeBlock

### Description
A code block with syntax highlighting.

### Class
`rich.markdown.CodeBlock`

### Constructor Parameters
- `lexer_name` (str)
- `theme` (str)

### Methods

#### create(markdown, token) (classmethod)
Factory to create markdown element.

**Parameters:**
- `markdown` (Markdown) – The parent Markdown object.
- `token` (Token) – A node from markdown-it.

**Returns:**
A new markdown element

**Return type:**
MarkdownElement
```

### lexer (property)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/syntax.rst

Property that returns the lexer for this syntax, or None if no lexer was found. It tries to find the lexer by name if a string was passed to the constructor.

```APIDOC
## lexer (property)

### Description
Property that returns the lexer for this syntax, or None if no lexer was found. It tries to find the lexer by name if a string was passed to the constructor.

### Returns
- **Lexer | None** - The lexer for this syntax, or None if no lexer was found.

```

--------------------------------

### Capture console output with capture() and get()

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Captures console output using the capture() context manager. Inside the with block, console.print writes to the capture; after exiting, capture.get() returns the string that would have been written to the terminal.

```python
from rich.console import Console
console = Console()
with console.capture() as capture:
    console.print("[bold red]Hello[/] World")
str_output = capture.get()
```

### Change spinner animation with spinner parameter

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/console.rst

Pass the spinner parameter to console.status() to change the spinner animation. The example uses the 'monkey' spinner.

```default
with console.status("Monkeying around...", spinner="monkey"):
    do_work()
```

### Prompt.ask with a default value

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/prompt.rst

Adds a default value that is returned if the user presses return without entering any text.

```python
>>> from rich.prompt import Prompt
>>> name = Prompt.ask("Enter your name", default="Paul Atreides")
```

--------------------------------

### Override highlighter per log message

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/logging.rst

Overrides the highlighter for a single log message by passing extra={"highlighter": None}, preventing automatic highlighting of numbers.

```python
log.error("123 will not be highlighted", extra={"highlighter": None})
```

--------------------------------

### set_window_title(title)

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/console.rst

Sets the title of the console terminal window. The title persists after the application exits, and some terminals may not support this feature.

```APIDOC
## set_window_title(title)

### Description
Set the title of the console terminal window. Note that there is no means within Rich of resetting the window title to its previous value, so the title will persist after the application exits. Some terminals may require configuration changes or may not support setting the title at all.

### Method
set_window_title

### Parameters
- **title** (str) - Required - The new title of the terminal window.

### Returns
- **bool** - True if the control code to change the terminal title was written, otherwise False. A return value of True does not guarantee that the window title has actually changed.
```

--------------------------------

### Traceback.extract

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/reference/traceback.rst

Classmethod that extracts traceback information from an exception and returns a Trace instance. Useful for constructing a Traceback manually.

```APIDOC
## Traceback.extract

### Description
Classmethod that extracts traceback information from an exception and returns a Trace instance. Useful for constructing a Traceback manually.

### Method
Classmethod

### Signature
`extract(exc_type, exc_value, traceback, *, show_locals=False, locals_max_length=10, locals_max_string=80, locals_max_depth=None, locals_hide_dunder=True, locals_hide_sunder=False, _visited_exceptions=None)`

### Parameters
- **exc_type** (Type[BaseException]) – Exception type.
- **exc_value** (BaseException) – Exception value.
- **traceback** (TracebackType) – Python Traceback object.
- **show_locals** (bool, optional) – Enable display of local variables. Defaults to False.
- **locals_max_length** (int, optional) – Maximum length of containers before abbreviating, or None for no abbreviation. Defaults to 10.
- **locals_max_string** (int, optional) – Maximum length of string before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to 80.
- **locals_max_depth** (int, optional) – Maximum depths of locals before truncating, or None to disable. Defaults to None.
- **locals_hide_dunder** (bool, optional) – Hide locals prefixed with double underscore. Defaults to True.
- **locals_hide_sunder** (bool, optional) – Hide locals prefixed with single underscore. Defaults to False.
- **_visited_exceptions** (Set[BaseException] | None) – (No description provided.)

### Returns
A Trace instance which you can use to construct a Traceback.

### Return Type
Trace
```

### Syntax CLI: highlight a file

Source: https://github.com/textualize/rich/blob/main/docs/source/syntax.rst

Use the command-line interface to syntax highlight a file. Run 'python -m rich.syntax syntax.py' to highlight the file 'syntax.py'.

```bash
python -m rich.syntax syntax.py
```
